v5: entropy regularizer — push scorer toward binary decisions\\n        ent_loss_val = 0.0\\n        if args.lambda_ent > 0 and policy._cached_logits:\\n            from acib.losses import ACIBLoss\\n            scorer_logits = torch.cat(policy._cached_logits, dim=1)\\n            ent_loss = ACIBLoss.entropy_regularizer(scorer_logits)\\n            total_loss = total_loss + args.lambda_ent * ent_loss\\n            ent_loss_val = ent_loss.item()\\n\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\n        total_loss.backward()\\n        if args.grad_clip > 0:\\n            nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(trainable_params, args.grad_clip)\\n        optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n\\n        # β warmup: hold at 0 for beta_warmup_steps, then linear ramp to beta over another beta_warmup_steps\\n        if global_step < args.beta_warmup_steps:\\n            beta_current = 0.0\\n        else:\\n            beta_current = args.beta * min(1.0, (global_step - args.beta_warmup_steps) / args.beta_warmup_steps)\\n        policy.acib_loss_fn.beta = beta_current\\n\\n        global_step += 1\\n\\n        # Save checkpoint\\n        if global_step % args.save_freq == 0 or global_step == args.steps:\\n            ckpt_dir = output_dir / f\\\"checkpoint-{global_step}\\\"\\n            ckpt_dir.mkdir(exist_ok=True)\\n            torch.save({\\n                \\\"step\\\": global_step,\\n                \\\"policy_state_dict\\\": policy.state_dict(),\\n                \\\"optimizer_state_dict\\\": optimizer.state_dict(),\\n                \\\"config\\\": vars(args),\\n            }, ckpt_dir / \\\"checkpoint.pt\\\")\\n            print(f\\\"  Saved checkpoint to {ckpt_dir}\\\")\\n\\n    total_time = time.time() - t0\\n    print(f\\\"\\\\nTraining complete: {args.steps} steps in {total_time/3600:.1f}h\\\")\\n\\n    if wandb_run:\\n        wandb_run.finish()\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    args = parse_args()\\n    train(args)\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 1.69}}"}

'1')\\nos.environ.setdefault('MUJOCO_GL', 'egl')\\nos.environ.setdefault('HF_HUB_OFFLINE', '1')\\n\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport sys\\nimport time\\nfrom pathlib import Path\\n\\nimport torch\\nimport torch.nn as nn\\n\\n# Add parent dir to path for acib imports\\nsys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent))\\n\\nfrom acib.config import ACIBConfig\\nfrom acib.acib_policy import ACIBPolicy\\n\\n\\n# Dataset→model image key remapping: LIBERO dataset uses image/image2,\\n# SmolVLA config expects camera1/camera2/camera3 (camera3 unused with empty_cameras=0)\\nIMAGE_KEY_MAP = {\\n    \\\"observation.images.image\\\": \\\"observation.images.camera1\\\",\\n    \\\"observation.images.image2\\\": \\\"observation.images.camera2\\\",\\n}\\n\\n\\ndef remap_image_keys(batch, key_map=IMAGE_KEY_MAP):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Rename dataset image keys to match model config expectations.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    for old_key, new_key in key_map.items():\\n        if old_key in batch and new_key not in batch:\\n            batch[new_key] = batch.pop(old_key)\\n    return batch\\n\\n\\ndef tokenize_batch(batch, tokenizer, max_length=48):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Tokenize task text into language tokens expected by SmolVLA forward.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    tasks = batch.get(\\\"task\\\")\\n    if tasks is None:\\n        return batch\\n    if isinstance(tasks, str):\\n        tasks = [tasks]\\n    encoded = tokenizer(\\n        list(tasks), max_length=max_length, padding=\\\"max_length\\\",\\n        truncation=True, return_tensors=\\\"pt\\\",\\n    )\\n    batch[\\\"observation.language.tokens\\\"] = encoded[\\\"input_ids\\\"]\\n    batch[\\\"observation.language.attention_mask\\\"] = encoded[\\\"attention_mask\\\"].bool()\\n    return batch\\n\\n\\n\\ndef load_normalization_stats(model_id):\\n    from huggingface_hub import hf_hub_download\\n    import safetensors.torch as st_safe\\n    normalizer_file = hf_hub_download(\\n        model_id,\\n        \\\"policy_preprocessor_step_5_normalizer_processor.safetensors\\\",\\n        local_files_only=True,\\n    )\\n    tensors = st_safe.load_file(normalizer_file)\\n    stats = {}\\n    for key in [\\\"observation.state\\\", \\\"action\\\"]:\\n        mean_k = f\\\"{key}.mean\\\"\\n        std_k = f\\\"{key}.std\\\"\\n        if mean_k in tensors and std_k in tensors:\\n            stats[key] = {\\\"mean\\\": tensors[mean_k], \\\"std\\\": tensors[std_k]}\\n    return stats\\n\\n\\ndef normalize_batch(batch, norm_stats):\\n    for key, stat in norm_stats.items():\\n        if key not in batch:\\n            continue\\n        tensor = batch[key]\\n        mean = stat[\\\"mean\\\"].to(device=tensor.device, dtype=tensor.dtype)\\n        std = stat[\\\"std\\\"].to(device=tensor.device, dtype=tensor.dtype)\\n        dim = min(tensor.shape[-1], mean.shape[0])\\n        batch[key] = (tensor[..., :dim] - mean[:dim]) / (std[:dim] + 1e-8)\\n    return batch\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"ACIB-S training\\\")\\n\\n    # Model / data\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--model_id\\\", type=str, default=\\\"lerobot/smolvla_libero\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--dataset_id\\\", type=str, default=\\\"lerobot/libero\\\")\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--data_root\\\", type=str, default=None,\\n                        help=\\\"Root directory for dataset cache/storage\\\")\\n\\n\\n\\n... [截断: 输出过长，省略 8047 字符] ...\\n\\n torch.autocast(device_type=\\\"cuda\\\", dtype=dtype):\\n            total_loss, loss_dict = policy(batch)\\n\\n        # v5: diversity regularizer\\n        div_loss_val = 0.0\\n        if args.diversity_lambda > 0 and policy._cached_logits:\\n            from acib.losses import ACIBLoss\\n            scorer_logits = torch.cat(policy._cached_logits, dim=1)\\n            div_loss = ACIBLoss.diversity_regularizer(scorer_logits)\\n            total_loss = total_loss + args.diversity_lambda * div_loss\\n            div_loss_val = div_loss.item()\\n\\n        # v5: entropy regularizer — push scorer toward binary decisions\\n        ent_loss_val = 0.0\\n        if args.lambda_ent > 0 and policy._cached_logits:\\n            from acib.losses import ACIBLoss\\n            scorer_logits = torch.cat(policy._cached_logits, dim=1)\\n            ent_loss = ACIBLoss.entropy_regularizer(scorer_logits)\\n            total_loss = total_loss + args.lambda_ent * ent_loss\\n            ent_loss_val = ent_loss.item()\\n\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\n        total_loss.backward()\\n        if args.grad_clip > 0:\\n            nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(trainable_params, args.grad_clip)\\n        optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n\\n        # β warmup: hold at 0 for beta_warmup_steps, then linear ramp to beta over another beta_warmup_steps\\n        if global_step < args.beta_warmup_steps:\\n            beta_current = 0.0\\n        else:\\n            beta_current = args.beta * min(1.0, (global_step - args.beta_warmup_steps) / args.beta_warmup_steps)\\n        policy.acib_loss_fn.beta = beta_current\\n\\n        global_step += 1\\n\\n        # Logging\\n        if global_step % 100 == 0:\\n            elapsed = time.time() - t0\\n            steps_per_sec = global_step / elapsed\\n            scorer_stats = policy.get_scorer_stats()\\n\\n            log_dict = {\\n                \\\"train/total_loss\\\": total_loss.item(),\\n                \\\"train/div_loss\\\": div_loss_val,\\n                \\\"train/ent_loss\\\": ent_loss_val,\\n                \\\"train/task_loss\\\": loss_dict.get(\\\"loss\\\", 0),\\n                \\\"train/consistency_loss\\\": loss_dict.get(\\\"acib_consistency\\\", torch.tensor(0)).item(),\\n                \\\"train/ib_loss\\\": loss_dict.get(\\\"acib_ib\\\", torch.tensor(0)).item(),\\n                \\\"train/contrastive_loss\\\": loss_dict.get(\\\"acib_contrastive\\\", torch.tensor(0)).item(),\\n                \\\"train/lr\\\": scheduler.get_last_lr()[0],\\n                \\\"train/steps_per_sec\\\": steps_per_sec,\\n                \\\"train/beta_current\\\": beta_current,\\n                **{f\\\"train/{k}\\\": v for k, v in scorer_stats.items()},\\n            }\\n\\n            print(f\\\"Step {global_step}/{args.steps} | \\\"\\n                  f\\\"loss={total_loss.item():.4f} \\\"\\n                  f\\\"task={loss_dict.get('loss', 0):.4f} \\\"\\n                  f\\\"ib={loss_dict.get('acib_ib', torch.tensor(0)).item():.4f} \\\"\\n                  f\\\"div={div_loss_val:.4f} \\\"\\n                  f\\\"ctr={loss_dict.get('acib_contrastive', torch.tensor(0)).item():.4f} \\\"\\n                  f\\\"β={beta_current:.4f} | \\\"\\n                  f\\\"{steps_per_sec:.1f} steps/s \\\"\\n                  f\\\"scorer_mean={scorer_stats.get('scorer/mean',0):.3f} std={scorer_stats.get('scorer/std',0):.3f}\\\")\\n\\n            if wandb_run:\\n                import wandb\\n                wandb.log(log_dict, step=global_step)\\n\\n        # tokenizer, max_length=48):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Tokenize task text into language tokens expected by SmolVLA forward.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    tasks = batch.get(\\\"task\\\")\\n    if tasks is None:\\n tensors = st_safe.load_file(normalizer_file)\\n    stats = {}\\n    for key in [\\\"observation.state\\\", \\\"action\\\"]:\\n        mean_k = f\\\"{key}.mean\\\"\\n        std_k = normalize_batch(batch, norm_stats):\\n    for key, stat in norm_stats.items():\\n        if key not in batch:\\n            continue\\n        tensor = batch[key]\\n        mean = stat[\\\"mean\\\"].to(device=tensor.device, dtype=tensor.dtype)\\n        std = stat[\\\"std\\\"].to(device=tensor.device, dtype=tensor.dtype)\\n        dim = min(tensor.shape[-1], mean.shape[0])\\n parser.add_argument(\\\"--wandb_run_name\\\", type=str, default=\\\"\\\")\\n\\n    # v5: diversity regularizer + stop-gradient\\n    parser.add_argument(\\\"--diversity_lambda\\\", type=float, v5: diversity regularizer\\n        div_loss_val = 0.0\\n        if args.diversity_lambda > 0 and policy._cached_logits:\\n            from acib.losses import ACIBLoss\\n            scorer_logits = torch.cat(policy._cached_logits, dim=1)\\n            div_loss = ACIBLoss.diversity_regularizer(scorer_logits)\\n            total_loss = total_loss + args.diversity_lambda * div_loss\\n            div_loss_val = div_loss.item()\\n\\n        # v5: entropy regularizer — push scorer toward binary decisions\\n        ent_loss_val = 0.0\\n        if args.lambda_ent > 0 and policy._cached_logits:\\n            from acib.losses import ACIBLoss\\n            scorer_logits = torch.cat(policy._cached_logits, dim=1)\\n            ent_loss = ACIBLoss.entropy_regularizer(scorer_logits)\\n            total_loss = total_loss + args.lambda_ent * ent_loss\\n            ent_loss_val = ent_loss.item()\\n\\n        optimizer.zero_grad()\\n        total_loss.backward()\\n        if args.grad_clip > 0:\\n            nn.utils.clip_grad_norm_(trainable_params, args.grad_clip)\\n        optimizer.step()\\n        scheduler.step()\\n\\n        # β warmup: hold at 0 for beta_warmup_steps, then linear ramp to beta over another beta_warmup_steps\\n        if global_step < args.beta_warmup_steps:\\n            beta_current = 0.0\\n        else:\\n            beta_current = args.beta * min(1.0, (global_step - args.beta_warmup_steps) / args.beta_warmup_steps)\\n        policy.acib_loss_fn.beta = beta_current\\n\\n        global_step += 1\\n\\n        # = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\\n    total = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters())\\n    print(f\\\"Parameters: {total:,} total, {trainable:,} trainable ({trainable/total:.1%})\\\")\\n\\n    return policy\\n\\n\\ndef build_dataloader(args, chunk_size=50):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build LeRobot dataloader for LIBERO with action chunks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from lerobot.datasets.lerobot_dataset import LeRobotDataset\\n    from lerobot.datasets.dataset_metadata import LeRobotDatasetMetadata as DatasetMetadata\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading dataset: {args.dataset_id}\\\")\\n    meta = DatasetMetadata(args.dataset_id, root=args.data_root)\\n    fps = meta.fps\\n    delta_timestamps = {\\\"action\\\": [i / fps for i in range(chunk_size)]}\\n    dataset = LeRobotDataset(\\n        args.dataset_id, root=args.data_root, delta_timestamps=delta_timestamps,\\n    )\\n    print(f\\\"Dataset: {len(dataset)} frames, fps={fps}, \\\"config.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(vars(args), f, indent=2)\\n\\n    wandb_run = setup_wandb(args)\\n\\n    policy = build_acib_policy(args).to(device)\\n    tokenizer = config=vars(args),\\n        )\\n        return run\\n    except ImportError:\\n        print(\\\"wandb not installed, skipping logging\\\")\\n        return None\\n\\n\\ndef build_acib_policy(args) -> ACIBPolicy:\\n    acib_config = ACIBConfig(\\n        hidden_dim=args.hidden_dim,\\n        alpha=args.alpha,\\n        beta=args.beta,\\n        mode=args.mode,\\n        top_k_ratio=args.top_k_ratio,\\n        condition_source=args.condition_source,\\n        freeze_vlm=True,\\n        stop_grad_task=args.stop_grad_task,\\n        lambda_contrastive=args.lambda_contrastive,\\n        conditioning_mode=args.conditioning_mode,\\n        freeze_action_expert=args.freeze_action_expert,\\n        train_scorer_only=args.train_scorer_only,\\n    )\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading base model: {args.model_id}\\\")\\n    policy = ACIBPolicy.from_pretrained(args.model_id, acib_config)\\n\\n    trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\\n    total = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters())\\n    print(f\\\"Parameters: {total:,} total, {trainable:,} trainable ({trainable/total:.1%})\\\")\\n\\n    return policy\\n\\n\\ndef build_dataloader(args, chunk_size=50):\\n    \\\"\\\"\\\"Build LeRobot dataloader for LIBERO with action chunks.\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n    from lerobot.datasets.lerobot_dataset import LeRobotDataset\\n    from lerobot.datasets.dataset_metadata import LeRobotDatasetMetadata as DatasetMetadata\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading dataset: {args.dataset_id}\\\")\\n    meta = DatasetMetadata(args.dataset_id, root=args.data_root)\\n    fps = meta.fps\\n    delta_timestamps = {\\\"action\\\": [i / fps for i in range(chunk_size)]}\\n    dataset = LeRobotDataset(\\n        args.dataset_id, root=args.data_root, delta_timestamps=delta_timestamps,\\n    )\\n    print(f\\\"Dataset: {len(dataset)} frames, fps={fps}, chunk_size={chunk_size}\\\")\\n\\n    dataloader = torch.utils.data.DataLoader(\\n        dataset,\\n        batch_size=args.batch_size,\\n        shuffle=True,\\n        num_workers=4,\\n        pin_memory=True,\\n        drop_last=True,\\n    )\\n    return dataloader\\n\\n\\ndef train(args):\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n    device = torch.device(f\\\"cuda:{args.gpu}\\\" if torch.cuda.is_available() else \\\"cpu\\\")\\n\\n    dtype_map = {\\n        \\\"float32\\\": torch.float32,\\n        \\\"bfloat16\\\": torch.bfloat16,\\n        \\\"float16\\\": torch.float16,\\n    }\\n    dtype = dtype_map[args.dtype]\\n\\n    output_dir = Path(args.output_dir)\\n    output_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    # Save config\\n    with open(output_dir / \\\"config.json\\\", \\\"w\\\") as f:\\n        json.dump(vars(args), f, indent=2)\\n\\n    wandb_run = setup_wandb(args)\\n\\n    policy = build_acib_policy(args).to(device)\\n    tokenizer = if step < args.warmup_steps:\\n            return step / max(1, args.warmup_steps)\\n        progress = (step - args.warmup_steps) / max(1, args.steps - args.warmup_steps)\\n        return 0.5 * (1.0 <details open><summary>🔧 Tool call — mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute · `toolu_014xEM8KPzQXuZhizJ4HuAGB`</summary>

```json
{
  "server": "westb-13953",
  "command": "source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/acib && python3 -c \"\nimport os\nos.environ['HF_HOME'] = '/root/autodl-tmp/.hf_cache'\nos.environ['HF_HUB_DISABLE_XET'] = '1'\nos.environ['HF_HUB_OFFLINE'] = '1'\nos.environ['MUJOCO_GL'] = 'egl'\n\nfrom acib.config import ACIBConfig\nfrom acib.acib_policy import ACIBPolicy\n\n# Test all three modes\nfor mode in ['legacy', 'scorer_only', 'connector_scorer']:\n    if mode == 'legacy':\n        config = ACIBConfig(hidden_dim=64, alpha=0.01, beta=0.01, train_scorer_only=True)\n    else:\n        config = ACIBConfig(hidden_dim=64, alpha=0.01, beta=0.01, freeze_mode=mode)\n\n    policy = ACIBPolicy.from_pretrained('lerobot/smolvla_libero', config)\n    trainable = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\n    total = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.parameters())\n    print(f'{mode:20s}: {trainable:>12,} trainable / {total:>12,} total ({trainable/total:.2%})')\n\n    # For connector_scorer, show breakdown\n    if mode == 'connector_scorer':\n        connector_params = sum(p.numel() for p in policy._connector.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\n        scorer_params = sum(p.numel() for p in policy.scorer.parameters() if p.requires_grad)\n        print(f'  connector: {connector_params:,}  scorer: {scorer_params:,}')\n    \n    del policy\n\" 2>&1",
  "account": "root",
  "warn": true
}
```

</details>

全部验证通过。总结：

### 修改结果

**config.py**: 新增 `freeze_mode` 字段，类型 `Literal["legacy", "scorer_only", "connector_scorer", "all_trainable"]`，默认 `"legacy"` 保持向后兼容。

**acib_policy.py**: `_setup_freeze` 新增 `freeze_mode != "legacy"` 分支，委托给 `_setup_freeze_by_mode`：
- `scorer_only`: 冻结全部 base_policy，仅训 scorer
- `connector_scorer`: 冻结全部 base_policy，解冻 connector + scorer
- `all_trainable`: 不冻结任何东西，仅确保 scorer 可训

**train_acib.py**: 新增 `--freeze_mode` CLI 参数，传入 ACIBConfig，日志输出。

**run_v7.2_connector_scorer.sh**: 训练脚本，lr=5e-5。

### 参数统计验证

| 模式 | Trainable | 比例 |
|------|-----------|------|
| legacy (scorer_only) | 123,009 | 0.03% |
| connector_scorer | 11,919,489 | 2.65% |

其中 connector_scorer = connector 11,796,480 + scorer 123,009，符合预期（~11.8M + ~0.1M）。
